### [1] [Exploiting Structure in the Boolean Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Problem: A Constraint Composite Graph-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10005)
*Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文围绕加权约束满足问题（WCSP）的“结构”利用展开，提出并系统研究约束复合图（CCG）表示，将WCSP统一转化为最小加权顶点覆盖（MWVC）问题，从而同时利用图结构与数值结构，并服务于经典与量子组合优化求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题广泛存在于系统设计、状态估计和预测等任务中，但传统专用算法通常仅适用于狭窄的问题子类，且不同研究领域容易重复发明相似技术。WCSP提供了统一建模框架，但其通用性可能带来求解困难。作者希望构建一种通用方法，使求解器能根据输入实例的结构自动呈现相应专用算法的优势。

Method: 论文以CCG为核心。CCG将WCSP中的变量及辅助变量构造成无向图，使原WCSP的求解等价于该图上的MWVC。研究从理论、实现和扩展三个层面推进：证明CCG除识别可解类别外的新增理论性质；开发高效实现并进行实验验证；为非布尔变量设计新的编码方式。基于CCG，作者进一步采用最大流在搜索前进行核化并固定部分变量的最优取值，改进min-sum消息传递，调用整数线性规划求解器，并映射至量子退火设备求解。

Result: 论文对三个核心问题给出肯定结论：CCG具有此前未充分揭示的理论优势；其在实践中可被高效实现并带来实际效用；该框架能够扩展到非布尔变量。实验和算法设计表明，CCG可用于预处理降维、增强消息传递、利用ILP技术，以及支持量子退火求解一般组合优化问题。

Conclusion: CCG为WCSP提供了统一的结构化求解视角，突破了仅利用宏观图结构或微观数值结构的传统分割。通过将WCSP归约为MWVC，它能够整合多类优化技术，并有望使通用求解器自动适配不同问题子类。更快的经典求解能力也可为评估量子退火方法的实际优势提供更强基线。

Abstract: What is "structure," and how can we exploit it in combinatorial optimization? Reasoning tasks such as system design, state estimation, and prediction can be cast as combinatorial optimization problems (COPs), traditionally attacked by dedicated algorithms that fail outside their narrow subclass, while communities working on equivalent problems reinvent each other's techniques.
  The weighted constraint satisfaction problem (WCSP) is a general framework that subsumes COPs from many communities and represents complex physical and non-physical systems, from spin glasses to social networks. Is such generality a curse? Our answer is to exploit "structure": a general-purpose WCSP algorithm should automatically imitate the specialized algorithm for whatever subclass its input belongs to.
  A WCSP has macro (graphical) structure, which variables interact, and micro (numerical) structure, how they interact. Separate schools of thought exploit one or the other, never both. The constraint composite graph (CCG), introduced in 2008, unifies them: it is an undirected graph over a WCSP instance's variables plus auxiliary ones, on which minimum weighted vertex cover (MWVC) solves the original instance. It is efficiently constructible, but largely unexploited.
  This dissertation answers three questions affirmatively. The CCG has theoretical advantages beyond identifying tractable classes (new properties proved here); it is practically useful (efficient implementation and experiments); and it extends promisingly to non-Boolean variables (new encodings). We use the CCG to (a) kernelize a WCSP instance, fixing optimal values of some variables by maxflow before search begins, (b) improve min-sum message passing, (c) exploit integer linear programming solvers, and (d) solve COPs on quantum annealers. Solving general COPs faster classically also yields better baselines for the debated quantum advantage.

</details>


### [2] [A Kruskal Decision Procedure for Intuitionistic Modal Logic IK4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10283)
*Mario Piazza*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文证明了 Simpson 直觉主义模态逻辑 IK 的可判定性，并构造了基于无切嵌套证明的有效判定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管固定终结公式后节点处可出现的输入/输出公式组合有限，但模态树深度仍可能无界；同时，Kruskal 定理仅保证嵌入关系是良拟序，尚不足以直接保证逆向规则搜索可计算。

Method: 作者以有根同胚嵌入为嵌套序列的预序：弱化对应增加输入公式，传递性对应将模态边扩展为非空路径。利用 Kruskal 定理获得良拟序性质；再通过有限支撑引理，证明最小前驱只需保留规则实际使用的位置、所选基元素的像及其连接分支点。结合有界规则实例的有效枚举，可计算所有最小前驱，并从初始序列出发迭代构造有限基表示的向上封闭集合。

Result: 该向上封闭集合序列单调增长且最终稳定；稳定集合恰为全部可证明的嵌套序列。因此，IK 的可证明性可判定。进一步地，所有可证明嵌套序列均可由有限多个无切证明经嵌入意义下的弱化获得。

Conclusion: 论文不仅给出 IK 可判定性的证明，还建立了有效的逆向饱和程序，并导出统一的证明高度上界：有限个代表性无切证明的最大高度即可界定所有可证明嵌套序列的证明高度。

Abstract: We prove decidability of Simpson's intuitionistic modal logic IK$ by working directly with cut-free nested proofs. Once the end formula is fixed, only finitely many combinations of input and output formulae can occur at a node, although the modal tree itself remains unbounded. We order these nested sequents by rooted homeomorphic embedding: weakening may add input formulae, while transitivity allows a modal edge to be stretched into a non-empty path.
  Kruskal's theorem makes rooted homeomorphic embedding a well-quasi-order, but does not by itself make backward application of the rules effective: an inference may still occur inside an arbitrarily large context. The finite-support lemma shows that a minimal predecessor need retain only the positions used by the inference, the images of the chosen basis elements, and the branch points joining them. Together with an effective enumeration of bounded rule instances, this bound makes the minimal predecessors computable. Backward closure from the initial sequents gives an increasing sequence of finitely based upward-closed sets. The sequence eventually stabilises, and its stable value is the set of provable nested sequents. At that point, finitely many cut-free proofs suffice: every other provable nested sequent is obtained from one of them by weakening along an embedding. Their maximum height gives a uniform proof-height bound.

</details>


### [3] [Mixed Choice Multiparty Session Types, Precisely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10704)
*Jake Masters,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文证明了支持会话委托、创建与交错的混合选择多方会话类型的子类型关系是精确的，即同时满足健全性与完备性，并实现了二次时间复杂度的相关检查算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多方会话类型中的子类型理论难以同时处理混合选择、会话交错及活性性质。论文旨在严格刻画“可安全替换”的子类型语义，并为自动验证提供可实现的判定方法。

Method: 作者为完整混合选择多方会话π演算构建通用类型系统以证明健全性；为证明完备性，提出三方锁这一用于描述交错会话中最小活性错误的结构，并通过构造调度器进程来穷尽检测子类型失败情形。随后将结果推广到一族混合选择多方会话类型，并实现子类型及类型上下文性质检查算法。

Result: 论文获得了子类型关系的健全性和完备性证明，将精确性结果扩展至更广泛的类型族；实现的算法可检查子类型、安全性、无死锁性和活性，并经优化后相对于状态空间与类型上下文规模具有二次时间复杂度。作者还使用文献中的混合选择案例进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为复杂多方并发协议中的精确子类型提供了理论基础，同时给出了可实践的自动化验证工具。其关键贡献是以三方锁和调度器构造解决会话交错下的完备性证明，并统一支持安全性与活性相关分析。

Abstract: A precise (sound and complete) subtyping relation $\leq$ specifies that $T'$ is a subtype of $T$ if and only if a program of type $T'$ can always safely replace a program of type $T$ without compromising the safety of a larger program. This paper formulates and proves preciseness of subtyping for mixed choice multiparty session types with session delegation, creation, and interleaving. We prove soundness by developing the first general type system for a full mixed choice multiparty session $π$-calculus. To prove completeness, we introduce the three-party lock, which is a minimal and general form of liveness error for handling interleaved sessions, and we establish a new proof technique based on a construction of scheduler processes which enable exhaustive detection for all failures of the subtyping relation. We then extend the preciseness results to a family of mixed choice multiparty session types. Algorithms for checking (1) subtyping and (2) safety, deadlock-freedom, and liveness of typing contexts are fully implemented and optimised to run in quadratic time with respect to the size of the state space and typing context, and have been evaluated with (mixed choice) case studies from the literature.

</details>


### [4] [Weighted First-Order Model Counting over Ordered Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10877)
*Jan Tóth,Qipeng Kuang,Kuncheng Zou,Václav Kůla,Yuyi Wang,Yuanhong Wang,Ondřej Kuželka*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文研究带权一阶模型计数（WFOMC）在有序域上的复杂度边界，证明二变量逻辑加入单个线性序公理后仍可多项式时间求解，并扩展到后继关系；但加入两个完整线性序会导致问题变为#P₁-困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WFOMC是统计关系学习、组合计数和图多项式中的基础计算问题。尽管二变量一阶逻辑的WFOMC通常可高效计算，直接在逻辑中编码线性序需要三个变量，从而破坏可处理性。论文旨在通过将线性序作为语言公理直接引入，刻画有序结构下可处理推理与计数的范围。

Method: 作者首先将一个谓词固定为定义域上的全序关系，并构造针对二变量片段的多项式时间WFOMC算法。随后研究后继关系：一类方法从线性序显式定义后继，另一类将后继关系隐式纳入公理并设计相应算法。最后通过复杂度归约分析两个线性序的情形，同时给出“一个线性序加另一个序的后继关系”的多项式时间算法。

Result: 带单个线性序公理的WFOMC可在定义域大小的多项式时间内计算；在该结构上加入后继关系后，该结论仍然成立。隐式后继编码在实验中始终优于显式定义方案，部分实例获得指数级运行时间改进。对于两个不同的完整线性序，二变量片段上的WFOMC是#P₁-困难；但一个完整线性序与另一关系的后继信息组合时，仍存在多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 论文精确推进了有序域WFOMC的可处理性边界：单一全序及其相关后继结构不会破坏二变量逻辑的多项式可计算性，而第二个完整全序会引入难解性。结果表明，后继关系比完整序关系表达能力更受限，因而可在保持高效计数的同时支持更丰富的序列化建模；第二个全序的哪些受限形式仍可处理，仍是开放问题。

Abstract: The Weighted First-Order Model Counting Problem (WFOMC) asks for the weighted sum of models of a first-order logical sentence over a domain. It is a fundamental problem in statistical relational learning, with applications extending to enumerative combinatorics and graph polynomials. Computing WFOMC for the three-variable fragment is $\mathsf{\#P}_1$-hard, whereas polynomial-time algorithms exist for the two-variable fragment and its extensions by cardinality constraints and counting quantifiers. In this work, we explore computing WFOMC in polynomial time over linearly ordered domains, enabling tractable reasoning across inference scenarios and combinatorial problems involving sequences. Because encoding a linear order in standard first-order logic requires three variables, negating our polynomial-time aspirations, we add a linear order axiom directly to the language. This forces one predicate to impose a total ordering on domain elements. We first prove that WFOMC with the linear order axiom can be solved in time polynomial in the domain size. We then extend this result to ordered domains with access to successor relations. While this holds when successors are explicitly defined via the linear order, we demonstrate an alternative implicit approach where successor relations are part of the axiom. This implicit method exhibits significantly better performance on all tested instances, sometimes providing exponential runtime improvements. Finally, we analyze scenarios with two distinct linear orders. We show that WFOMC over the two-variable fragment with two linear orders is $\mathsf{\#P}_1$-hard. However, we develop a polynomial-time algorithm for WFOMC with one linear order and a successor relation of another, pushing the intractability barrier further, yet still leaving the question of how close to a second full linear order one can get.

</details>


### [5] [FormaTheoria: Constructing Large-Scale Lean Theories from Mathematical Literature $-$ Toward the Formalization of the Classification of Finite Simple Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10894)
*Tianjiao Nie,Ao Zhang,Yusen Tang,Damiano Testa,Shing-Tung Yau,Peng Li,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出FormaTheoria：一个面向大规模数学理论重建的端到端AI辅助形式化工作流，并以有限群分类相关理论为案例，在Lean中完成延伸至Bender--Suzuki定理的机器检查形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学形式化通常聚焦孤立命题，而高级数学理论分散于多种文献，存在隐含依赖、原始资料错误、语义保真困难及不同来源表述不一致等问题。作者旨在建立可持续重建完整理论体系的方法，而非仅自动翻译单个定理。

Method: FormaTheoria协调资料获取、形式化编码、证明构造、递归依赖发现、独立审查和跨来源协调，并记录命题来源、保护已批准声明。其共享智能体框架通过工具调用、上下文压缩、审查门控终止、按章节保留源文献上下文，以及依赖感知的批处理并行化，支持长周期任务执行。

Result: 作者构建了经Lean机器检查的有限群理论开发，覆盖Feit--Thompson奇数阶定理、Glauberman Z*定理、Brauer--Suzuki定理，并推进至Bender--Suzuki定理。代码及构建过程的实证分析表明，所识别的四类困难在实践中确实存在，相应工作流组件对处理这些困难具有实际作用。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合语言模型智能体、形式验证、结构化审查和显式依赖管理，可以从分散文献中重建具有重要数学意义且高度相互依赖的形式化理论。FormaTheoria为后续持续推进有限群分类相关理论的形式化提供了基础。

Abstract: Large-scale formalization of advanced mathematics requires more than translating individual statements: it must reconstruct a coherent theory distributed across heterogeneous sources. This process raises four challenges: discovering implicit dependencies, correcting source defects, preserving semantic fidelity, and reconciling cross-source misalignments. We present FormaTheoria, an end-to-end, AI-assisted workflow that coordinates source acquisition, formalization, proof construction, recursive dependency discovery, independent review, and reconciliation, while preserving provenance and protecting approved declarations. A shared agent framework supports long-horizon execution through tool use, context compaction, review-gated termination, section-level source context, and dependency-aware batch parallelization. Applying FormaTheoria to major components of the Classification of Finite Simple Groups (CFSG), we construct a machine-checked Lean development extending through the Bender--Suzuki theorem and encompassing the Feit--Thompson Odd Order Theorem, Glauberman's $Z^*$ theorem, and the Brauer--Suzuki theorem. This development verifies an extensive body of deeply interdependent finite-group theory while providing a foundation for continuing the CFSG formalization. An empirical analysis of the code and recorded construction process supports the practical relevance of the identified challenges and illustrates the roles of the corresponding workflow components. Together, these results demonstrate how AI-assisted workflows can reconstruct mathematically significant formal theories from distributed literature by combining language-model agents with formal verification, structured review, and explicit dependency management.

</details>

### [6] [Evaluating Shrinking (Experience Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09935)
*Alperen Keles,George Miao,Leonidas Lampropoulos*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了三种 Haskell 性质测试框架——QuickCheck、Hedgehog 与 Falsify——的收缩（shrinking）能力，发现 QuickCheck 的结构化收缩通常速度更快，且最终反例质量具有竞争力；集成式收缩并不必然带来效率或效果优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性质测试常通过随机生成输入发现失败，而收缩过程负责将复杂失败样例简化为可调试的最小反例。现有研究与实践通常关注缺陷发现能力，却较少定量衡量收缩算法本身的质量、速度及其权衡，因此需要建立可比较的评估方法。

Method: 研究在四个 ETNA 工作负载上比较 QuickCheck、Hedgehog 和 Falsify，并覆盖基于类型、基于 API 及正确性构造式等多类生成器。有效性通过与穷举搜索得到的真实最小反例计算树编辑距离来衡量；成本则使用收缩总耗时及单位收缩进展所需时间进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，QuickCheck 的结构化收缩在多数工作负载中具有更高速度，同时生成的最终反例在质量上仍可与其他框架竞争。Hedgehog 与 Falsify 所采用的集成式收缩机制没有稳定地表现出更优的性能或更接近全局最小反例的效果。

Conclusion: 收缩算法应被作为性质测试框架的独立核心能力进行量化评估，而非仅以缺陷发现率间接判断。未来收缩设计与实验应同时报告反例最小化质量、运行成本和生成器类型带来的影响；集成式设计不能被预设为优于结构化收缩。

Abstract: Property-based testing frameworks rely on shrinking to turn noisy random failures into counterexamples that developers can debug. Although bug-finding performance is routinely measured, shrinking itself is rarely evaluated quantitatively. We present an experience report on evaluating shrinking across three Haskell frameworks: QuickCheck, Hedgehog, and Falsify. The comparison spans four ETNA workloads and several generator families, including type-based, API-based, and correct-by-construction generators. We measure both effectiveness, using tree edit distance to a ground-truth minimum found by exhaustive search, and cost, using shrink time and time per unit of shrinking progress. Across these workloads, QuickCheck's structural shrinking is usually faster and remains competitive on final counterexample quality; integrated shrinking does not by itself guarantee a performance or effectiveness advantage. We discuss what these results imply for future evaluations and designs of shrinking algorithms.

</details>

### [7] [N2NMatcher: Towards Inlining-Resilient Binary Decomposition and Module Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10043)
*Ang Jia,He Jiang,Zhipeng Yang,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出N2NMatcher，一种面向函数内联场景的程序级二进制代码相似性分析框架，通过识别稳定的函数锚点进行模块划分，并利用图嵌入完成模块匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依据函数调用图将二进制程序划分为模块，再通过模块内函数进行匹配。但编译器的函数内联会改变函数调用关系和函数语义边界，导致传统模块分解与匹配方法的鲁棒性下降。

Method: 作者首先实证研究不同编译配置下是否存在可作为稳定模块边界的函数。随后，N2NMatcher使用分层图神经网络预测这些函数锚点；模型联合编码由操作码序列、控制流结构及函数调用上下文构成的ACFG-FCG表示。系统以锚点为边界分解二进制程序，并通过学习得到的模块图嵌入表示进行模块相似性匹配。

Result: 实验表明，相较于已有方法，N2NMatcher在二进制模块分解质量和模块匹配准确率上均有提升，尤其能够缓解函数内联导致的结构变化问题。

Conclusion: N2NMatcher通过学习内联条件下仍相对稳定的函数边界，将模块划分与图表示学习结合，提高了程序级二进制代码相似性分析的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Program-level Binary Code Similarity Analysis (BCSA) aims to identify semantically similar code regions across binary programs, serving as a fundamental technique for software plagiarism detection, vulnerability search, and malware analysis. Existing approaches often decompose binaries into modules following the structure of function call graphs (FCGs) and then match these modules by their contained functions. However, function inlining changes both FCG structures and binary function semantics, making existing decomposition and module matching methods less effective.
  In this work, we propose N2NMatcher, an inlining-resilient framework for binary decomposition and module matching. We first conduct an empirical study to examine whether binaries still contain functions that provide stable module boundaries across compilation settings. N2NMatcher learns to predict such functions as anchors using a hierarchical graph neural network that encodes binary ACFG-FCG representations built from opcode sequences, control-flow structures, and FCG calling context. It then performs anchor-bounded decomposition and matches the generated modules using learned module graph embeddings. Experimental results show that N2NMatcher improves both the decomposition quality and module matching accuracy compared to existing works, enabling more effective program-level BCSA.

</details>


### [8] [Comprendia: AI-Augmented Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10290)
*Costain Nachuma,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comprendia 是一款面向 Java 程序理解的 Eclipse 插件，将依赖关系可视化、基于大语言模型的代码解释、克隆检测与 CVE 风险提示整合到同一个交互式图界面中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在理解大型代码库时，通常需要在调用关系、代码解释、重复代码识别和依赖安全风险之间频繁切换。现有 LLM 代码解释往往缺乏可审计的上下文选择机制，导致解释依据不透明、结果难以复现。该工作旨在以统一的依赖图作为共同基础，提升程序理解过程的可见性、可追溯性和开发者控制能力。

Method: 系统构建了支持多种边类型的结构依赖图，并提供实时搜索和多种图布局。其核心方法是图感知被调用者剪枝（GACP）：根据图距离、继承关系折叠以及边类型权重，从调用上下文中筛选相关被调用者，再将这些可见图节点对应的代码信息构造成 LLM 提示词。系统还在图上叠加克隆检测结果，以识别重复代码并建议向父类提取；同时接入 OSV.dev，在图中展示已知漏洞相关的 CVE 风险。

Result: 作者在一个包含已知代码克隆和漏洞的 Java 项目上演示了 Comprendia。结果表明，统一图模型能够同时支撑结构导航、上下文约束的 LLM 解释、重复代码发现和安全风险定位。GACP 生成的上下文选择可在不同 LLM 系列间复用，并能够追溯到开发者界面中可见的具体图节点。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种以交互式依赖图为中心的 IDE 程序理解方案。其主要贡献在于将 LLM 解释的上下文选择与开发者可见的图结构绑定，从而增强解释过程的可审计性与可复现性；结合克隆和漏洞叠加层后，系统可支持更综合的代码维护任务。摘要中的证据主要来自演示案例，尚需进一步的用户研究、准确性评估和与其他上下文检索策略的对比实验来验证实际效益。

Abstract: Comprendia is an Eclipse plugin that integrates structural dependency visualization with LLM-powered code explanation on a shared interactive graph for Java program comprehension. The tool rests on four pillars: (1) a multi-edge-type dependency graph with live search and multiple layouts; (2) LLM explanations grounded in Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP), an auditable strategy that selects relevant callees using the same graph the developer navigates; (3) a clone-detection overlay that highlights duplication and suggests extract-to-parent refactoring opportunities; and (4) a CVE risk overlay powered by OSV.dev. GACP uses graph distance, inheritance collapse, and edge-type weighting to produce prompts that are reproducible across LLM families and traceable to visible graph nodes. We demonstrate Comprendia on a Java project containing known clones and vulnerabilities, showing how the unified graph substrate supports comprehension while keeping the developer in control. Screencast: https://youtu.be/1wlh_RYehzA

</details>


### [9] [Exploring Semantic Stability Across Reviews in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10101)
*Lucas Ciziks,Paulo Meirelles,Marco Aurélio Gerosa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文以函数为单位追踪补丁评审过程中的代码演化，发现表面上的极高相似度主要由大量未被修改的函数造成；在仅分析实际修改过的函数后，代码语义目的总体仍被保留，但相似度指标对局部关键修改的敏感性仍值得进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常只考察最终合并版本，缺少与首次提交版本的直接比较，因此难以准确量化评审对代码的实际影响。作者旨在揭示补丁多轮评审中函数级代码变化的真实程度，并检验高相似度是否意味着功能意图确实未变。

Method: 作者从Linux IIO子系统的补丁历史中提取10,117条函数轨迹，即跟踪同一补丁系列中某个函数在各编号修订版本里的变化。研究采用函数相似度分数衡量版本间差异，并以无关函数对的相似度作为基线。分析分别考察全部轨迹与仅包含实际文本修改的轨迹，同时控制不同评审轮次中未修改函数比例造成的组成效应。

Result: 全部轨迹的相似度接近100%，但其中75.3%的函数在版本间没有文本改动，其固定的100%相似度显著抬高了总体结果。仅考察发生真实编辑的函数时，平均相似度为0.990，仍高于无关函数对的0.909基线，表明函数的语义目的通常得到保留。变化主要集中在第一轮评审；这一现象主要源于后续轮次中未被修改的函数更多，而非后期编辑本身更保守。控制该组成差异后，轮次效应仍具有统计可检测性，但效应量较小。

Conclusion: 代码评审确实会修改部分函数，但从文本相似度看，多数已修改函数仍与原始版本高度接近。评审轮次的表面差异不能直接解释为编辑强度递减，必须控制未修改函数比例。论文的核心局限是：近乎饱和的相似度究竟代表语义意图被保留，还是现有度量无法识别局部但重要的修改，仍未得到充分区分；后续工作应结合更具语义感知能力的指标与人工验证。

Abstract: Code review is credited with substantially changing a patch's code between its first submission and the version that eventually lands. However, prior work typically studied only the final merged patch without comparing it to the first submission. We present a function-level measurement that tracks 10,117 trajectories (each function followed across the numbered revisions of one patch series) through the patch history of the Linux IIO subsystem, comparing similarity scores against unrelated function pairs as a baseline. A naive reading yields near-total similarity, but this is largely an artifact of composition: 75.3% of tracked trajectories are never textually modified between versions, contributing a trivial 100% similarity that inflates the headline. Restricting to the trajectories with a real edit, semantic purpose is still largely preserved (mean similarity 0.990 vs. a 0.909 baseline), but drift appears to concentrate in the first review round mainly because later rounds contain more functions that nobody touched, not because edits become more conservative over time. After controlling for it, a statistically detectable but small residual effect remains. This points to an open question: whether near-ceiling similarity reflects preserved purpose or a measurement tool that cannot detect the significance of small, localized edits. We present this work as a first look and outline next steps.

</details>


### [10] [One Recipe, Many Harnesses: What Self-Evolution Encodes Across Languages and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10178)
*Siqi Yang,Qianlan Yang,Yu-Xiong Wang,Saurabh Pujar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统分析自演化编程智能体框架，发现其提升主要来自对基础模型可恢复执行缺陷的补偿，而非不可解释的基准投机；框架具有可迁移的通用核心，但语言生态相关部分仍需针对性重新演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常只报告自演化框架的总体性能增益，尚不清楚演化后的提示词、工具和记忆究竟编码了基准特定策略、编程语言工程知识，还是对基础模型能力缺口的补偿。论文旨在分离并解释这些来源，以提高演化产物的可理解性与可迁移性。

Method: 作者在八种编程语言构成的Multi-SWE-Bench与三个基础模型的组合网格上，固定同一套演化流程进行比较。每次框架编辑均由类型化失败信号触发，并记录为可证伪契约，从而能够在演化后追溯每项修改的作用。实验比较最小初始框架、人工设计的mini-SWE-agent脚手架及演化框架，并进一步考察跨语言共享、迁移、蒸馏与本地重演化效果。

Result: 演化循环在多数模型—语言组合中提高了留出测试集的解决率，但存在两个无显著收益区域。性能增益与可恢复执行缺陷高度对应：当缺陷量接近零时，收益也接近零；不同组合中的主导缺陷不同。各语言演化出的框架共享抽象策略，例如围绕失败诊断和修复进行闭环控制，但其具体工具、依赖与工作流几乎不重合，反映出明显的语言生态差异。共享核心可跨语言迁移，并可蒸馏为统一框架；然而生态相关的增益部分难以直接迁移或蒸馏，仍需要在目标语言环境中重新演化。

Conclusion: 自演化框架应被理解为一种可解释的“补偿层”：它缩小了模型策略潜在能力与实际执行表现之间的差距，其结构同时受语言工程需求和模型行为缺陷塑造。该结论表明，构建通用框架时可复用跨语言的诊断与控制核心，但要获得完整性能，必须保留或重新学习目标语言生态所需的专用机制。

Abstract: Self-evolving harnesses are closed-loop systems in which an agent inspects its own rollouts and edits its prompts, tools, and memory. They reliably improve coding agents in evaluations, but prior work reports aggregate gains rather than analyzing what the evolved artifacts encode. It therefore remains unclear whether they encode benchmark-specific adaptations, language-specific engineering knowledge, or compensation for limitations of the underlying model. We disentangle these factors by holding an evolution recipe fixed across a grid of eight programming languages (Multi-SWE-Bench) and three base models, and analyzing the resulting harnesses. The recipe routes every edit through a typed failure signal and records it as a falsifiable contract, making each modification attributable after evolution. Four findings emerge. (1)The loop improves held-out solve rates over both a minimal seed and the manually designed mini-SWE-agent scaffold in most cells, but with two null regions. (2)Gains compensate recoverable execution defects, where defect mass is near zero, and gain is near zero; which defect dominates is cell-specific. A harness closes the gap between what a policy can do and what it does. (3)Evolved harnesses share an abstract playbook across languages but instantiate it with almost disjoint language ecosystem machinery. (4)The shared core transfers and can be distilled into one universal harness, while an ecosystem margin resists both and requires native re-evolution. Together, these results recast the evolved harness as a legible compensation layer, shaped jointly by the language's engineering demands and the model's behavioral gaps, rather than an opaque benchmark-tuned scaffold.

</details>


### [11] [Conflict Extraction in Probabilistic Datalog Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10755)
*Siyu Chen,Chungha Sung,Xuyang Li,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出PPProbe，用于从概率Datalog静态分析结果中提取彼此无法共同成立的告警集合，以减少开发者处理逻辑上不可能同时出现的告警组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Datalog可为静态分析告警提供概率排序，但单个告警的边缘概率并不能保证多个告警可在同一可能世界中共同满足。因此，分析输出中可能包含各自合理但相互矛盾的告警，造成无效排查工作。

Method: 作者将告警间的不一致形式化为最小不可满足子集（MUS），并设计专用冲突提取器PPProbe。PPProbe不以通用MUS枚举为目标，而是利用Datalog推导图结构：通过面向高可能性冲突的搜索策略缩小候选范围，并采用自底向上的不可满足性推断对搜索空间进行剪枝。

Result: 在覆盖功耗侧信道分析、并发缺陷检测、语义差异分析和贝叶斯网络推理的70个基准上，PPProbe的吞吐量相比先进MUS枚举器提高2.5至24倍。其发现的冲突可保守估计可减少的误报量，平均能够过滤47.7%的相互不一致告警。

Conclusion: PPProbe证明了针对概率Datalog推导结构进行专门优化，能够比通用MUS枚举方法更高效地识别告警冲突，并显著降低由不可共现告警导致的排查负担。

Abstract: Probabilistic extensions of Datalog enable static analyses such as pointer analysis, data race detection, and side-channel analysis to rank alarms by likelihood, but this added expressiveness also introduces a new challenge absent from deterministic analyses: the final output may contain alarms that are individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent, because marginal probabilities do not guarantee joint satisfiability. As a result, developers may spend effort investigating combinations of alarms that can never co-occur in any possible world. We address this problem by formalizing such inconsistencies as minimal unsatisfiable subsets (MUSes) and introducing PPProbe, a conflict extractor specialized for probabilistic Datalog analyses. Rather than improving MUS enumeration in general, PPProbe exploits the structure of Datalog derivation graphs to guide the search toward likely conflicts and prune the search space through bottom-up UNSAT inference. We evaluate PPProbe on 70 benchmarks from power side-channel analysis, data race detection, semantic diffing, and Bayesian-network inference. The results show that PPProbe achieves 2.5 to 24 times higher throughput than state-of-the-art MUS enumerators, and that the conflicts it identifies yield a conservative estimate of false-positive reduction, filtering out an average of 47.7% of mutually inconsistent alarms.

</details>


### [12] [Bias Smells in AI Software Development: Recognizing Potential Sources of Fairness Debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10248)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过一个组织内四个AI项目的案例研究，识别出六类可能预示公平性债务的“偏差异味”，为在软件质量保障中及早发现公平风险提供了可观察的实践线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已讨论技术债务与社会债务，但AI系统开发决策可能持续累积并放大偏差，其形成的公平性债务缺少来自实践场景的识别证据。该研究旨在理解从业者如何在开发过程中察觉这类风险来源。

Method: 研究采用探索性案例研究，覆盖同一组织的四个AI项目。研究者从25名专业人员处收集半结构化访谈和开放式问卷数据，并结合内部沟通渠道观察及项目文档。数据分析采用迭代式定性编码、分析备忘录和持续比较方法，以归纳重复出现的风险信号。

Result: 研究归纳出六类偏差异味：语境过度简化、数据集失衡、指标不足、临时性测试、个人多样性意识不足，以及团队构成同质化。这些指标同时涉及技术实践和人员因素，表明项目中存在可能引入、维持或强化偏差，并进一步形成公平性债务的条件。

Conclusion: 偏差异味将传统软件异味概念扩展至公平性问题，提供了一套可用于早期识别公平风险的质量保障框架。其主要价值在于将抽象的公平性债务转化为开发团队可观察、可讨论和可干预的具体指标；但由于研究仅覆盖一个组织中的四个项目，后续仍需在更多组织、领域和开发流程中验证其普适性与可操作性。

Abstract: Context: Fairness debt arises from AI development shortcomings that may lead to societal harms. While technical and social debt concern software design decisions and team dynamics, respectively, fairness debt captures the long-term consequences of development decisions that may reinforce bias and inequities in AI-based software systems. Despite growing attention to AI fairness, limited evidence exists on how practitioners recognize potential sources of fairness debt during development. Aim: This study investigates the indicators practitioners recognize as signaling potential sources of fairness debt in AI-based software projects. Method: We conducted an exploratory case study of four AI projects within one organization. Data were collected from 25 professionals through semi-structured interviews and open-ended questionnaires, complemented by observation of internal communication channels and project documentation, and analyzed using iterative qualitative coding, memoing, and constant comparison. Results: We identified six recurring indicators, termed bias smells: Context Oversimplification, Dataset Imbalance, Metrics Inadequacies, Ad hoc Testing, Individual Diversity Unawareness, and Homogeneous Team Composition. These smells span technical and human aspects of software development and signal conditions that may introduce or reinforce bias and contribute to fairness debt. Conclusion: Bias smells extend the software smell paradigm to fairness and provide a foundation for incorporating fairness into software quality assurance through observable indicators.

</details>


### [13] [Enhancing Reliability of Symbolic Execution Tools for Smart Contract Analysis through Rule-Based False Positive Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10265)
*Muhammad Ali Hassan Ahmad,Muhammad Hashim Ali,Muhammad Ali Amer,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Muhammad Hassan,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文聚焦以太坊智能合约符号执行工具 Mythril 的误报问题，提出基于规则的改进方法，在不降低真实漏洞检出能力的前提下显著减少误报，从而提升工具可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约安全分析缺乏统一标准，现有工具虽能发现潜在漏洞，但常产生大量误报。人工核查误报会消耗审计资源，并可能掩盖真正高风险问题。因此，除检出率外，降低误报率是评估和改进漏洞检测工具的关键目标。

Method: 作者首先使用 Mythril 生成的漏洞报告说明误报现象的普遍性，随后分析误报产生的根本原因。基于这些分析结果，论文设计了一套规则驱动的方法，用于补充或约束 Mythril 的漏洞判定逻辑；该方法针对影响最显著的漏洞类型实现，并通过实验评估其对误报与真实漏洞检测能力的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的规则能够显著减少 Mythril 报告中的误报；同时，真实漏洞的检测能力未受到明显损害。这说明规则能够有效地区分部分真实可利用问题与表面上满足告警条件但实际不可利用的情况。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，针对符号执行工具中的具体误报成因引入领域规则，是提升智能合约漏洞检测实用性和可信度的有效路径。其主要贡献在于将评估重点从单纯发现告警扩展到告警准确性，但摘要未提供具体漏洞类别、规则细节、数据集规模及量化改进幅度，仍需结合全文判断方法的泛化性。

Abstract: A blockchain is a decentralized, secure ledger system that enables transparent and immutable record-keeping, essential for trust and security in digital transactions. Smart contracts are self-executing agreements encoded on a blockchain, enabling different parties to fulfill the terms of the agreement automatically. These contracts trigger corresponding actions when conditions are met, ensuring decentralized and transparent transactions. Writing reliable smart contracts is challenging due to the lack of standardization. To find security vulnerabilities, tools based on various approaches, including symbolic execution, are used. However, these tools often report a large number of false positives, raising concerns about their reliability. The time and effort spent investigating false positives diverts resources from addressing actual vulnerabilities. Therefore, such tools must also be evaluated according to the rate of false positives they exhibit. More importantly, the algorithms and heuristics used by the tools must be enhanced to distinguish between true vulnerabilities and false alarms. In this paper, we first demonstrate the prevalence of false positives in vulnerability reports generated by Mythril, a symbolic execution-based analysis tool for Ethereum smart contracts. We analyze the root causes of these inaccuracies and devise a rule-based approach based on the gained insight to reduce false positives. We implement our rules for the most impactful vulnerabilities in Mythril and assess the effectiveness of our approach. Our results show a significant reduction in false positives without compromising the detection of true vulnerabilities, thus enhancing the tool's reliability.

</details>


### [14] [Does the way we write a theory change the program an LLM builds from it? A prospective randomized study of renderer format in LLM theory-to-program translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10314)
*Andre Panossian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该预注册随机实验检验理论内容的呈现格式（结构化干预契约 vs. 连贯散文）是否会系统性改变大语言模型生成的可执行程序。总体证据不支持该假设：主要终点未达标，同一理论账户的行为可识别性接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将语言理论转化为程序时，模型必须自行决定变量、干预和交互关系，因此理论的表达形式可能影响最终形式化结果。研究旨在以可审计、随机化的方式，界定“规格呈现格式”对LLM理论到程序转换的影响边界。

Method: 研究采用前瞻性预注册随机设计，以16个独立分配位将32个配对渲染槽分配至两种格式。两个固定版本的LLM对5个匿名理论账户进行转换，生成320个预先授权、单次采样的稀疏二次语言程序。确定性评估器测量原子有限差分响应（H1）和混合交互响应（H2）；通过跨模型配对距离缩减与封闭集合内同账户可识别性两个主要终点，结合精确随机化推断及27项预注册支持标准，在符号线性与幅度秩两类分析流程中评估。

Result: H1和H2均得到预注册结论“NOT_SUPPORTED”。108项标准评估中仅19项通过；同一理论账户的可识别性AUC为0.469至0.523，接近机会水平，显著低于预注册阈值0.80。尽管H2的一个配对距离终点在符号线性流程中发生变化并通过多重比较校正，但对应的幅度秩分析未达到预设效应量下限，因此不满足联合支持规则。

Conclusion: 在该实验设定、模型快照、理论样本和程序语言范围内，呈现格式未能产生预先预测的、一致的、跨理论族不变且可分类的行为几何结构。研究的主要贡献是提供了规格格式效应缺乏稳健支持的实证边界，并给出可复核的随机化设计、数据集和软件框架，用于后续研究LLM的可执行形式化能力。

Abstract: A verbal theory does not run: translating it into an executable model requires choices about variables, interventions, and interactions. We tested whether the presentation of otherwise identical theoretical content systematically changes the programs produced by large language models. In a prospective preregistered randomized experiment, 16 independent assignment bits allocated 32 paired renderer slots between a structured intervention contract and connected prose. Two pinned LLM snapshots translated five anonymous theoretical accounts, yielding 320 preauthorized single-shot programs in a frozen sparse quadratic language. A deterministic evaluator measured atomic finite-difference responses (H1) and mixed interaction responses (H2). Two primary endpoints assessed cross-model matched-distance reduction and closed-set same-account identifiability, with exact randomization inference and 27 preregistered support criteria evaluated across signed-linear and magnitude-rank pipelines. Both H1 and H2 returned the registered verdict NOT_SUPPORTED; only 19 of 108 criterion evaluations passed. Same-account identifiability remained near chance (AUC 0.469-0.523, against a registered 0.80 threshold). One H2 matched-distance endpoint moved and survived multiplicity correction in the signed-linear pipeline, but the corresponding magnitude-rank result missed the registered effect-size floor, so it did not satisfy the joint support rule. Thus renderer format did not produce the uniform, family-invariant, classifiable behavioral geometry predicted in advance. The result places a concrete boundary on specification-format effects in LLM theory-to-program translation and provides a fully auditable randomized design, datasets, and software for studying executable formalization.

</details>


### [15] [Do Personalized Skills Help Coding Agents? An Empirical Study of Developer Interaction Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10319)
*Shuyan Huang,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究如何从开发者与编程智能体的历史交互中提炼可复用的个性化技能，并检验其对后续任务的帮助。结果显示，个性化技能仅带来小幅且不稳定的提升；跨开发者汇总的通用技能效果最稳定、总体最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编程智能体参与复杂软件工程任务，开发者会在持续协作中形成偏好。若智能体能够记录并复用这些偏好，可减少重复纠正并改善协作体验。然而，现有技能研究主要面向任务本身，尚不清楚从交互历史中蒸馏出的开发者特定偏好能否迁移到未来任务。

Method: 作者提出开发者偏好提取框架：首先通过基于规则的引导生成初始个性化技能，再结合交互证据进行细化；随后使用可复现的回放评测框架，并引入基于交互轨迹条件化的大语言模型开发者模拟器评估技能效果。实验覆盖13名开发者的206个真实开发者—智能体会话，并比较个性化技能、无技能、跨用户通用技能及其他用户技能等设置。

Result: 相较无技能基线，个性化技能的收益较小且不同任务间不一致。由多位开发者经验汇聚而成的通用技能带来了最大且最稳定的改善。进一步分析发现，当某开发者偏好在历史记录中出现频率更高、且历史中包含多个与未来任务相关的实例时，个性化技能更可能有效。

Conclusion: 开发者特定偏好并非在所有场景中都能可靠提升编程智能体表现；其有效性依赖于偏好的重复出现与历史示例对未来任务的相关性。相比之下，可跨开发者迁移的过程性知识更稳健。该研究为个性化编程智能体的适用边界、技能构建方式及评测设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have rapidly evolved from code-completion tools into solvers of complex software engineering tasks. As developers collaborate with coding agents over time, their preferences emerge through repeated interactions and can be used to adapt agent behavior to better meet individual developers' needs. Capturing and reusing these preferences may reduce repeated corrections and improve developer-agent collaboration. Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for transferring experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing work primarily focuses on task-specific skills, and it remains unclear whether developer-specific skills distilled from interaction histories can generalize to future tasks. We propose a framework for extracting reusable developer preferences from interaction traces. It first generates personalized skills through rule-based bootstrapping and evidence-grounded refinement, and then evaluates them using a reproducible replay framework with an interactive, trajectory-conditioned LLM-based human developer simulator. We conduct an experiment on 206 real-world developer-agent sessions from 13 developers and compare personalized skills against no-skill, generic-skill, and other-user-skill baselines. Personalized skills provide small and inconsistent improvements over the no-skill baseline, whereas generic skills pooled across developers achieve the largest and most consistent gains. Further analysis suggests that personalized skills become more effective when developer preferences appear frequently, particularly when their histories contain multiple examples relevant to future tasks. These findings provide empirical insights into when developer-specific personalization is effective and demonstrate that broadly transferable procedural knowledge can be more robust than developer-specific preference signals.

</details>


### [16] [Persistent Recursive Worlds Enable Autonomous Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10450)
*Beichen Huang,Zhenyu Liang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 EvoX Genesis，一种以持久化软件项目而非持久化智能体为中心的长周期开发框架。该框架通过版本历史、仓库路径和递归委派维持开发连续性，使有限生命周期的智能体能够接力完成大型软件工程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体软件系统通常依赖持久会话、记忆或管理器维持上下文，但智能体本身容易中断、替换或遗忘。论文旨在解决跨越多个智能体生命周期的长期软件开发问题，将持续性从智能体转移到可验证、可演化的代码仓库与版本历史。

Method: Genesis 将项目组织为持久化递归世界：每个局部世界由已接受的版本和仓库路径定义；有限生命周期智能体在局部范围内提出修改；任务可通过递归委派在不同路径间传递；只有通过接受流程的修改才会推进全局版本历史。作者在编译器从零构建、智能体替换后的持续开发，以及 Fortran 科学计算模块重实现三类场景中评估该机制。

Result: 在无既有编译器实现的仓库中，Genesis 使用 DeepSeek V4 Flash 在超过120小时内构建了约25万行受跟踪代码的 Rust C 编译器，归档超过1000个智能体工作片段，模型令牌成本约44美元；该编译器通过完整 c-testsuite，并通过大多数 LLVM 和 Csmith 测试。在另一编译器项目中，使用 GLM 5.2 时，即使多次替换智能体，系统仍保持完整测试性能。对于13个 MESA 模块，Genesis 将超过10万行 Fortran 重实现为近9万行 Rust 工作区，在六个数值负载上获得1.55至6.87倍的中位加速。

Conclusion: 论文表明，长周期智能体软件工程可以围绕持久化项目状态、路径化任务边界和可接受版本演进来组织，而无需依赖单个持续存在的智能体。实验结果支持该架构在大型代码生成、开发接力和性能导向重构中的可行性，但摘要未充分说明接受机制的具体标准、人工干预程度及不同模型和任务上的泛化边界。

Abstract: Complex software systems develop over timescales that exceed the lifespan of any individual coding agent. Most agentic software systems preserve continuity through persistent sessions, memories, managers or shared context. We introduce EvoX Genesis (hereafter, Genesis), which instead makes the software project persistent while allowing local agents to remain finite-lived. Genesis represents software as a persistent recursive world: each local world is situated by an accepted version and a repository path, finite-lived agents propose local changes, recursive delegation moves work across paths, and only accepted consequences advance the persistent version history. We evaluate this organization across formation, continuation and redevelopment. Starting from a repository with no compiler implementation, Genesis used DeepSeek V4 Flash to build a Rust-based C compiler with about 250k tracked lines; the run lasted over 120 hours, archived over 1,000 agent episodes and incurred only US$44 in model-token charges. The compiler passed the complete c-testsuite and most LLVM and Csmith tests. In a separate compiler world generated with GLM 5.2, development continued after repeated agent replacement while retaining full test performance. Genesis also reimplemented 13 MESA modules with over 100k Fortran lines as a Rust workspace with nearly 90k Rust lines; across six numerical workloads, it achieved median speedups of 1.55--6.87x. These results show that long-horizon software development can be organized around a persistent project rather than a persistent agent.

</details>


### [17] [CausalRepair: Bridging the Causality Gap in Large Language Model-Based Automated Program Repair via Dual-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10613)
*Linhao Wu,Yizhou Chen,Zhen Yang,Pengyu Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CausalRepair，一种基于“最小因果上下文”的对话式自动程序修复框架。该方法通过结合静态切片与动态切片，为大语言模型提供与故障根因紧密相关的精简上下文，在Defects4J基准上正确修复313个缺陷，并将单个缺陷的平均修复成本降至0.029美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自动程序修复方法高度依赖输入上下文，但测试上下文可能存在噪声或信息缺失，而静态分析得到的源码上下文通常包含未执行或无关代码。这种上下文与真实故障因果链之间的偏差会误导模型定位根因，降低补丁生成质量，因此需要构造更小、更准确且具有因果解释力的修复上下文。

Method: CausalRepair以最小因果上下文为核心，采用双切片策略构建修复提示：第一，使用上下文感知静态切片提纯测试语义，识别与失败断言和测试执行逻辑相关的关键部分；第二，使用基于执行轨迹的动态切片提取源码中实际运行且影响故障的依赖语句。系统将两类切片结果组合为紧凑的因果相关上下文，并通过对话式、迭代式过程引导大语言模型分析故障、生成补丁和改进候选修复。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2、V2.0及Defects4J-Trans上的实验使用DeepSeek-V3作为底层模型。CausalRepair在Defects4J上正确修复313个缺陷，优于ReinFix和TSAPR等已有方法。除修复数量提升外，方法还实现了较低的成本，平均每个缺陷的修复成本为0.029美元，表明精简因果上下文能够同时改善修复有效性与推理经济性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将自动程序修复中的上下文选择问题明确建模为因果上下文构造问题。双切片机制减少了测试与源码中的无关信息，使大语言模型能够更聚焦于实际执行路径和故障依赖链。实验结果表明，该设计在修复能力和成本控制上具有竞争力；但摘要未说明补丁过拟合、动态切片覆盖不足、跨语言泛化能力及不同模型条件下的稳定性，这些仍需通过更全面的消融实验和人工补丁质量评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has recently benefited from Large Language Models (LLMs), yet their effectiveness heavily depends on repair context. Existing LLM-based APR methods suffer from a causality gap: test contexts can be noisy or incomplete, while source contexts derived from static analysis often contain irrelevant and unexecuted code, misleading LLMs from identifying the true root cause. To address this issue, we propose CausalRepair, a conversation-driven APR framework based on minimal causal context, i.e., the essential dependencies required to explain a failure. CausalRepair employs a dual-slicing strategy: context-aware static slicing purifies test semantics, while execution-trace-based dynamic slicing captures precise runtime dependencies in source code. Together, they construct compact, causally relevant contexts to guide iterative repair. We evaluate CausalRepair on Defects4J V1.2, V2.0, and Defects4J-Trans using DeepSeek-V3. CausalRepair correctly fixes 313 bugs on Defects4J, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches such as ReinFix and TSAPR, while reducing the average repair cost to $0.029 per bug.

</details>


### [18] [A Study of Cursorrules Files in GitHub Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10622)
*Shuang Sun,Jafar Akhoundali,Arina Kudriavtseva,Sengim Karayalcin,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对 Cursor 编辑器中的配置型提示文件进行大规模实证研究，发现 .cursorrules 文件自 2024 年中期快速出现，主要被小型、低活跃度、单维护者仓库采用；其内容集中于代码质量、工程实践、项目结构与可维护性，而安全相关指导相对较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示工程研究主要关注对话式提示词，较少考察以配置文件形式长期存在、可复用且可版本管理的提示文件。随着 AI 辅助编程工具同时使用动态对话提示和静态提示配置，理解此类文件的采用模式、内容特征及维护方式具有必要性。

Method: 研究者从 11,427 个 GitHub 仓库收集了 12,110 个 .cursorrules 文件，分析其分布、演化和维护情况；此外，随机抽取 65 个文件进行定性分析，构建包含 65 个编码项的代码本，用于归纳开发者在编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全考虑等方面的表达方式。

Result:  .cursorrules 的使用在 2024 年中期后迅速增长，但采用者主要是小规模、提交活动较低、由单一维护者管理的仓库，表明其当前应用更偏向实验性或轻量级项目。文件内容最常涉及代码质量规范、工程实践、项目结构与配置、可维护性要求；安全主题出现频率较低。研究还发现，旧版 .cursorrules 与当前标准 .mdc 文件在主题和内容上具有延续性。

Conclusion: 配置型提示文件是 AI 辅助编程中值得独立研究的提示载体。当前 Cursor 提示文件的实践显示，开发者主要借其固化工程规范和项目上下文，但安全指导尚未得到充分覆盖。由于 .cursorrules 与 .mdc 存在内容连续性，针对旧格式获得的经验在一定程度上可迁移至当前标准格式。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for communicating with AI agents, and they directly influence the quality and reliability of AI-generated code. As AI-assisted programming becomes widely adopted, modern tools increasingly combine dynamic conversational prompts with static configuration-like prompt files. Despite the growing focus on prompt engineering, prior research has primarily focused on conversational prompts, while prompt files remain understudied.
  To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of configuration prompt files in Cursor, a widely used AI-assisted code editor. We collect and analyze over 12,110 .cursorrules files from 11,427 GitHub repositories to characterize their distribution, evolution, and maintenance. Complementing this, we perform qualitative analysis on a random sample of 65 prompt files and develop a 65-code codebook capturing how developers express programming intent, project context, engineering practices, and security considerations.
  Our results show that .cursorrules files emerged rapidly from mid-2024. Their adoption is concentrated in small-scale, low-activity, single-maintainer repositories, suggesting toy projects rather than professional development. The content of prompt files is dominated by guidance on code quality and engineering practices, project structure and configuration, and maintainability, while security-related content appears less frequently. Our analysis shows that there is a continuity of themes and topics between the now-legacy .cursorrules files and the current standard .mdc files.

</details>


### [19] [LLM Ensemble Fault Classification for Automotive HiL Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10710)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向汽车硬件在环（HiL）验证的可解释多大语言模型集成框架，用于传感器级故障分类。该方法通过紧凑证据表示与置信度加权投票提升诊断准确率、排序质量、概率校准与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车HiL测试会产生大量多变量记录，传统人工审查成本高，规则方法适应性有限。现有机器学习和深度学习方法通常依赖大量标注数据、跨工况泛化能力不足，且预测依据不透明。因此，论文旨在构建一种能够支持稳健、可信且工程师可理解的故障诊断方案。

Method: 框架首先将故障注入测试记录转换为紧凑的证据表示，再输入多个异构大语言模型进行故障类别判断。系统比较不同单模型表现，并采用基于模型置信度的加权投票构建Top-3和Top-5集成。实验覆盖汽油发动机和电动车HiL系统、3种驾驶场景及10类单一故障，并使用Top-1准确率、宏平均F1、MCC和Brier分数评估分类性能与概率校准。

Result: 单模型中，Mistral Small 24B表现最佳，达到0.903的Top-1准确率、0.887的MCC，以及最低的0.102 Brier分数。最终Top-3集成由Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B和Phi-4 14B组成，在场景平均结果上达到0.917 Top-1准确率、0.913宏平均F1和0.902 MCC，并在测试的集成策略中具有最佳校准表现。Top-5集成未超过Top-3，说明有效的模型互补性比增加模型数量更关键。

Conclusion: 多LLM协同推理能够有效支持汽车HiL验证中的传感器级故障分类。该研究表明，选择互补性强的模型并进行置信度加权集成，可同时改善准确性、鲁棒性、概率可信度和诊断可解释性；但摘要未说明证据表示的具体构造、模型推理成本及面对复合故障和未见故障类型时的泛化能力，这些仍是实际部署中的关键验证点。

Abstract: Automotive HiL validation generates large multivariate test recordings whose analysis remains challenging due to manual review effort, rule-based limitations, and the need for explainable diagnostic decisions. Recent machine-learning and deep-learning approaches have improved fault diagnosis, but they often require large labelled datasets, generalise poorly across operating conditions, and provide limited insight into their predictions. This paper proposes an explainable multi-LLM ensemble framework for sensor-level fault classification in automotive validation. The framework uses compact evidence representations of fault-injection recordings and combines the outputs of heterogeneous large language models to improve diagnostic robustness, ranking quality, confidence reliability, and interpretability. The approach is evaluated on gasoline-engine and electric-vehicle HiL systems across three driving settings and ten single-fault classes. Among the individual models, Mistral Small~24B provides the strongest overall single-model trade-off, achieving 0.903 Top-1 accuracy, 0.887 MCC, and the lowest Brier score of 0.102. The final Top-3 ensemble combines Mistral Small~24B, Qwen2.5~32B, and Phi-4~14B using confidence-weighted voting, improving the scenario-averaged results to 0.917 Top-1 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1, and 0.902 MCC, while also providing the best calibration among the tested ensemble strategies. A Top-5 ensemble does not improve over the Top-3 configuration, indicating that model complementarity is more important than ensemble size. The results show that coordinated multi-LLM reasoning can support robust, calibrated, and engineer-interpretable fault classification for automotive HiL validation.

</details>


### [20] [Extending Triple Graph Grammars to Formalize Complex View Definitions on Families of Models - Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10785)
*Lars König,Jens Kosiol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文为NeoJoin视图定义语言补充基于三元图文法（TGG）的形式语义，并提出TGG规则的“跳过语义”，以支持重叠查询的便捷翻译，从而实现模型与视图之间具备形式保证的自动化、增量同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向复杂系统的视图驱动开发要求视图语言同时具备易用性、任务适应性与严格形式语义。缺少精确语义会使多个视图中产生的工作难以被受控集成为一致的整体模型；已有TGG方案对于NeoJoin中的重叠查询缺乏方便的翻译支持。

Method: 作者延续此前对NeoJoin形式化的工作，将其视图定义映射到基于TGG的语义框架。针对重叠查询的表达与执行难点，扩展TGG规则的常规执行方式，引入“跳过语义”：规则可依据当前应用上下文跳过其规定的部分动作，而非必须完整执行全部动作。

Result: 该形式化框架为模型和视图之间构造自动化、增量式同步过程提供了基础，并保留TGG方法的行为正确性保证。跳过语义填补了TGG在翻译NeoJoin重叠查询时的表达便利性缺口，同时构成对TGG表达能力和可用性的进一步案例验证。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是以TGG为NeoJoin建立可用于同步的形式语义，并通过上下文相关的规则跳过机制增强TGG对重叠查询的支持。这使视图定义语言能够兼顾工程可用性与形式严谨性，并促进复杂系统开发中多视图成果的一致集成。

Abstract: View-based development is an important technique to manage complexity and facilitate cooperation in the development of modern and complex systems of systems. This approach places high demands on view definition languages used in the development process. First, such a language needs to be approachable and extremely versatile so that developers from diverse backgrounds are able to use it and can define views for a diverse set of different tasks. Yet, the language also needs to have a precise and formal semantics so that it is possible to integrate the work performed on various views in a controlled manner to obtain a coherent overall system. In this paper, we continue previous work of equipping the NeoJoin view definition language with a formal semantics that is based on triple graph grammars (TGGs). This paves the way for obtaining automated and incremental synchronization procedures between models and views that come with high formal guarantees for their behavior. Simultaneously, our work serves as a further case study of the expressivity and usability of TGGs. We identify one gap, namely convenient support for the translation of overlapping queries from the view definition language, and tackle that gap by introducing a skip semantics for TGG rules, i.e., allowing to skip certain actions a rule prescribes, depending on the application context.

</details>


### [21] [From Pattern Detection to Composition Analysis in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10882)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Erico Augusto da Silva,Anthony Accioly,Higor Amario de Souza,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估了量子软件模式挖掘管线 qpa，通过扩展匹配渠道、术语扩展和组件调用图分析，系统研究开源量子项目中模式的采用与组合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件模式虽可为量子程序开发提供高层抽象，但其在实际项目中的采用情况、模式共现原因及框架内部调用对检测结果的影响，仍缺乏充分的实证证据。

Method: 作者基于已有的61项模式目录、框架组件—模式知识库和代码挖掘工具，新增两类匹配通道及词汇扩展步骤；在未纳入知识库的Qrisp框架上进行定量准确率评估；构建关联模式组件调用关系的组合图并存入图数据库，用于分析模式组装、共现及直接开发者调用比例。知识库覆盖5个来源中的286个框架组件，并通过LLM集成辅助分类新提取组件。

Result: 在80个开源项目中，已观察到23种模式均有实际出现。针对Qrisp的评估显示，加入词汇扩展后微平均F1达到0.712，而未使用该步骤时为0.449，表明该扩展显著提升了跨框架检测准确性。组合图进一步支持对框架内模式组合关系、模式共现机制和间接调用影响的分析。

Conclusion: qpa及其公开的知识库和模式使用数据集，为可复现地研究量子软件模式的实际采用、跨框架迁移、组合结构与演化提供了基础设施；词汇扩展是提升未知框架中模式识别性能的关键机制。

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>


### [22] [GitSkills: A Dataset of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10906)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Daniel Graziotin,Matteo Vaccargiu,Marco Ortu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 GitSkills：一个面向语言模型代理技能文件的大规模数据集，收集了公共代码仓库中的 3,797,117 个 SKILL.md 文件，用于研究代理技能的采用、复用、结构、维护与安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理技能以自然语言指令为主，由模型在运行时概率性选择，缺少编译器、类型检查、中心化注册表和包管理机制，因此其编写、传播、复用及维护模式尚不明确。现有软件工程数据集未系统记录这一新型软件工件，形成了实证研究缺口。

Method: 作者于 2026 年 7 月从 282,200 个公共代码仓库采集 SKILL.md 文件，保留每个文件出现位置对应的仓库、路径和内容哈希；将完全相同的文件按内容聚合，并为每个内容组选择一个代表文件补充全文、解析后的前置元数据、所在文件夹内容及仓库元数据。对于部分样本，数据集还提供文件提交历史。最终以单个独立 SQLite 文件发布。

Result: 数据集包含 3,797,117 个文件实例，并聚合为 1,877,981 个不同内容，说明技能文件既存在广泛分布，也存在显著的复制传播现象。其文件级出现记录与内容级去重表示可同时支持扩散路径、重复使用和内容演化分析。

Conclusion: GitSkills 为代理技能这一缺乏统一分发与验证机制的软件工件提供了大规模、可查询的研究基础设施。该数据集可支撑对技能生态的采用规律、文本与目录结构、作者和仓库特征、复制复用、历史维护，以及潜在安全问题开展可复现的实证研究。

Abstract: An agent skill is a folder containing a SKILL.md file with instructions for a language-model agent, optionally accompanied by scripts and reference files. The agent loads the skill when it judges that a task matches the skill description. Anthropic introduced the format in October 2025 as an open specification. Nine months later, we find that skill files in the millions sit in public GitHub repositories. Skills are unlike the artifacts the SE research community usually mines: they are written mainly in natural language, a model selects them probabilistically at run time, and no compiler or type checker verifies the selection. They also have no central registry or package manager, so they spread by copying folders between repositories. How developers write, reuse, and maintain skills is therefore an empirical question, and no existing dataset records this population. We present GitSkills, a dataset of 3,797,117 SKILL.md files collected from 282,200 public repositories in July 2026. The dataset retains every file occurrence with its repository, path, and content hash. It groups identical files into 1,877,981 distinct contents and enriches one representative per group with the full text, parsed front matter, folder contents, repository metadata, and, for a subset, the commit history of the file. A single self- contained SQLite file supports research on the adoption, reuse, structure, authorship, maintenance, and security of agent skills.

</details>


### [23] [Understanding the Architecture of Coding Agents: An Exploratory Study Using a Research Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10934)
*Marco Tulio Valente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统梳理了编程智能体的核心架构，并提出面向研究与教学的轻量级开源智能体Ark及其配套基准ArkBench。实验显示，Ark在10项软件维护与演化任务中解决了8项，表明简化架构仍可具备较强实用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程智能体已成为AI辅助软件开发的重要交互形式，但其内部架构缺少类似编译器或操作系统那样的系统化描述。这种知识缺口限制了研究者、教育者和开发者对智能体设计、执行机制及性能差异的理解与复现。

Method: 作者首先归纳编程智能体的主要架构组件，说明各组件的职责、相互作用和执行流程；随后实现最小化开源原型Ark，保留现代编程智能体的关键机制，同时强调简单性与可解释性；构建包含10个代表性软件维护和演化任务的轻量基准ArkBench；最后使用gpt-5.4-mini评测Ark，并依据已有架构分类体系，将Ark与先进编程智能体进行架构比较。

Result: Ark在ArkBench的10项任务中成功完成8项，并且令牌消耗较低。该结果说明，在适当的任务范围内，具有核心规划、工具调用与执行机制的简化智能体架构可以取得较好的软件工程任务表现。论文还通过架构分类比较，定位了Ark与现有先进系统之间的共同机制及可能差异。

Conclusion: 该工作同时贡献了编程智能体的架构性说明、可供分析和复现的最小开源实现Ark，以及轻量评测集ArkBench。其主要价值在于降低编程智能体研究与教学的进入门槛，并为后续研究架构设计、组件消融、执行策略和评测方法提供实践基础。

Abstract: Coding agents have rapidly emerged as the primary interface for AI-assisted software development. However, despite their growing adoption, relatively little is known about their internal architecture, and no systematic architectural description comparable to those available for compilers or operating systems currently exists. This paper addresses this gap by documenting the main architectural components of coding agents, explaining their responsibilities, interactions, and execution flow. To support this effort, we also present Ark (Agent Research Kit), a minimal open-source coding agent designed for research and education that preserves the essential architectural mechanisms of modern coding agents while emphasizing simplicity and clarity. We also introduce ArkBench, a lightweight benchmark comprising ten representative software maintenance and evolution tasks. Using gpt-5.4-mini, Ark successfully solved 8 of the 10 tasks while requiring modest token consumption. Finally, we compare the architecture of Ark with those of state-of-the-art coding agents using a recently proposed architectural taxonomy. We hope that both Ark and ArkBench provide a practical foundation for teaching, research, and experimentation on coding agents.

</details>


### [24] [Agentic Configuration Management (ACM): A Reference Configuration Model for Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11166)
*Audrey Quessada-Vial*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“智能体配置管理”（ACM）框架，旨在为异构智能体系统提供统一、可版本化、可审计且与具体框架无关的配置治理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMOps与AgentOps工具虽支持编排和可观测性，但缺少能够统一描述、版本控制和治理多种智能体、提示词、工具、模型、技能、策略及工作流配置的共同模型。不同框架和运行环境中的配置演化进一步加剧了复现、审计、依赖分析与互操作的困难。

Method: ACM定义了类型化且可独立版本化的智能体配置项、不可变修订与基线、配置与运行时分离、生命周期和保障语义、依赖感知的影响传播以及运行时溯源机制。该方法通过语义投影将不同框架的原生配置归一化为规范化“配置图”，并在该图上实施统一治理语义。作者实现了Python参考系统，并为LangGraph、CrewAI和OpenAI Agents SDK开发适配器；评估包括27个治理场景和9个定量影响传播案例。影响传播被形式化为有限格上的单调传播，并证明其收敛性、终止性及最小不动点的唯一性。

Result: 在所评估的配置范围内，三个框架经语义投影后产生治理等价的ACM表示，并获得可复现的治理结果。形式化分析表明，影响传播计算能够终止并收敛到唯一的、位于初始影响赋值之上的最小不动点。这些结果支持ACM在跨框架配置复现、审计、依赖影响分析和互操作方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将异构智能体系统的配置治理抽象为独立于执行框架的统一模型，并以原型实现和跨三类框架的实验验证其可行性。其结论具有明确边界：证据仅覆盖所选框架、配置样例和评估场景；实际大规模部署中的性能开销、语义投影完整性、复杂动态运行时变更及更广泛生态兼容性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly composed of heterogeneous agents, prompts, tools, models, skills, composite subsystems, policies, and execution workflows whose configurations evolve across frameworks and runtime environments. Existing LLMOps and AgentOps platforms support orchestration and observability but do not provide a common configuration-governance model for representing and governing these systems as coherent, versioned configurations.
  This paper introduces Agentic Configuration Management (ACM), a framework-independent governance and configuration reference model for heterogeneous agentic systems. ACM combines typed and independently versioned Agentic Configuration Items, immutable revisions and baselines, explicit configuration-runtime separation, lifecycle and assurance semantics, dependency-aware impact propagation, and runtime provenance. Heterogeneous native configurations are normalized through semantic projection into a canonical Configuration Graph on which common governance semantics operate.
  We provide a Python reference implementation with adapters for LangGraph, CrewAI, and the OpenAI Agents SDK. The evaluation combines 27 governance scenarios with nine quantitative impact-propagation cases. For the evaluated configurations, the three frameworks yield governance-equivalent ACM representations and reproducible governance outcomes after projection. The impact semantics are formalized as monotone propagation over a finite lattice, establishing convergence, termination, and uniqueness of the least fixed point above the initial impact valuation. These results provide evidence that common governance semantics can support reproducibility, auditability, dependency analysis, and interoperability across heterogeneous agentic execution abstractions within the evaluated scope.

</details>
